  "content": "import { Container, getContainer } from \"@cloudflare/containers\";\n\ninterface Env {\n  DBT_CONTAINER: DurableObjectNamespace<DbtContainer>;\n  DBT_ARTIFACTS: R2Bucket;\n  DATABRICKS_HOST: string;\n  DATABRICKS_HTTP_PATH: string;\n  DATABRICKS_TOKEN: string;\n}\n\nexport class DbtContainer extends Container<Env> {\n  defaultPort = 8080;\n\n  // dbt run は数分かかるため、十分なアイドル時間を確保\n  sleepAfter = \"5m\";\n\n  envVars = {\n    DATABRICKS_HOST: this.env.DATABRICKS_HOST,\n    DATABRICKS_HTTP_PATH: this.env.DATABRICKS_HTTP_PATH,\n    DATABRICKS_TOKEN: this.env.DATABRICKS_TOKEN,\n  };\n\n  override onStart() {\n    console.log(\"dbt-runner container started\");\n  }\n\n  override onStop() {\n    console.log(\"dbt-runner container stopped\");\n  }\n\n  override onError(error: unknown) {\n    console.error(\"dbt-runner container error:\", error);\n  }\n}\n\nexport default {\n  async fetch(request: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {\n    const url = new URL(request.url);\n    const path = url.pathname;\n\n    // Health check\n    if (path === \"/health\") {\n      return Response.json({ status: \"ok\" });\n    }\n\n    // Get or create container instance\n    const container = getContainer(env.DBT_CONTAINER, \"dbt-runner-main\");\n\n    // dbt commands: POST /run, /seed, /test, /build\n    if (request.method === \"POST\" && [\"/run\", \"/seed\", \"/test\", \"/build\"].includes(path)) {\n      const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));\n      const response = await container.fetch(`http://container${path}`, {\n        method: \"POST\",\n        headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" },\n        body: JSON.stringify(body),\n      });\n      const result = (await response.json()) as Record<string, unknown>;\n\n      // dbt run/build の成功後、アーティファクトを R2 に保存\n      if ((path === \"/run\" || path === \"/build\") && result.returncode === 0) {\n        await saveArtifactsToR2(container, env.DBT_ARTIFACTS);\n      }\n\n      return Response.json(result);\n    }\n\n    // アーティファクト取得 (R2 から)\n    if (path.startsWith(\"/artifacts/\")) {\n      const key = path.replace(\"/artifacts/\", \"\");\n      const object = await env.DBT_ARTIFACTS.get(`dbt-core/${key}`);\n      if (!object) {\n        return Response.json({ error: \"not found\" }, { status: 404 });\n      }\n      return new Response(object.body, {\n        headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" },\n      });\n    }\n\n    return Response.json({ error: \"not found\" }, { status: 404 });\n  },\n};\n\nasync function saveArtifactsToR2(\n  container: DurableObjectStub<DbtContainer>,\n  bucket: R2Bucket\n) {\n  try {\n    const response = await container.fetch(\"http://container/artifacts\", {\n      method: \"POST\",\n    });\n    const data = (await response.json()) as {\n      artifacts: string[];\n      data: Record<string, unknown>;\n    };\n\n    const timestamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, \"-\");\n\n    for (const [filename, content] of Object.entries(data.data)) {\n      // 最新版を保存\n      await bucket.put(`dbt-core/latest/${filename}`, JSON.stringify(content), {\n        httpMetadata: { contentType: \"application/json\" },\n      });\n\n      // タイムスタンプ付きアーカイブ\n      await bucket.put(\n        `dbt-core/history/${timestamp}/${filename}`,\n        JSON.stringify(content),\n        { httpMetadata: { contentType: \"application/json\" } }\n      );\n    }\n\n    console.log(\n      `Saved ${data.artifacts.length} artifacts to R2: ${data.artifacts.join(\", \")}`\n    );\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error(\"Failed to save artifacts to R2:\", error);\n  }\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

TypeCheck 通りました。全体の構成をまとめます。

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
- Cloudflare Containers は **Durable Objects の拡張**。Worker → DO → Container の3層構造
- Worker Secrets (DATABRICKS_TOKEN 等) は `envVars` で Container に渡す。Container 内の Python プロセスは通常の `os.environ` で読める
- R2 バインディングは **Worker 側**で持つ。Container がアーティファクトを HTTP で返し、Worker が R2 に保存する設計
- `sleepAfter: "5m"` でアイドル5分後にコンテナ停止 → コスト節約
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

## 作成したファイル

```
transform/dbt-runner/
├── Dockerfile              # dbt-databricks + core プロジェクト
├── server.py               # Container 内 HTTP サーバー (dbt コマンド実行)
├── src/index.ts            # Worker (ルーティング + R2 アーティファクト保存)
├── wrangler.jsonc           # Containers + R2 バインディング設定
├── tsconfig.json
├── package.json             # pnpm
└── .gitignore
```

## API エンドポイント

| メソッド | パス | 動作 |
|---------|------|------|
| GET | `/health` | ヘルスチェック |
| POST | `/run` | `dbt run` → 成功時アーティファクトを R2 保存 |
| POST | `/seed` | `dbt seed` |
| POST | `/test` | `dbt test` |
| POST | `/build` | `dbt build` (seed+run+test) → R2 保存 |
| GET | `/artifacts/latest/manifest.json` | R2 からアーティファクト取得 |

## デプロイ前に必要なこと

1. **R2 バケット作成**: `wrangler r2 bucket create dbt-artifacts`
2. **Secrets 設定**:
   ```bash
   wrangler secret put DATABRICKS_HOST
   wrangler secret put DATABRICKS_HTTP_PATH
   wrangler secret put DATABRICKS_TOKEN
   ```
3. **デプロイ**: `pnpm deploy` (Docker ビルド + Worker デプロイ)

#### Configuration  
You can simply configure your Worker and your Container(s) in your Wrangler configuration file:  
   * [  wrangler.jsonc ](#tab-panel-1546)  
   * [  wrangler.toml ](#tab-panel-1547)  
```  
{  
  "name": "container-starter",  
  "main": "src/index.js",  
  "containers": [  
    {  
      "class_name": "MyContainer",  
      "image": "./Dockerfile",  
      "instances": 5  
    }  
  ],  
  "durable_objects": {  
    "bindings": [  
      {  
        "class_name": "MyContainer",  
        "name": "MY_CONTAINER"  
      }  
    ]  
  },  
  "migrations": [  
    {  
      "new_sqlite_classes": [  
        "MyContainer"  
      ],  
      "tag": "v1"  
    }  
  ],  
}  
```  
```  
name = "container-starter"  
main = "src/index.js"  
[[containers]]  
class_name = "MyContainer"  
image = "./Dockerfile"  
instances = 5  
[[durable_objects.bindings]]  
class_name = "MyContainer"  
name = "MY_CONTAINER"  
[[migrations]]  
new_sqlite_classes = [ "MyContainer" ]  
tag = "v1"  
```

TypeScript

<title></title>
<text>
### Configuration

Your [Wrangler configuration file](/workers/wrangler/configuration/) defines the configuration for both your Worker and your container:

* [  wrangler.jsonc ](#tab-panel-4164)
* [  wrangler.toml ](#tab-panel-4165)

```

{

  "containers": [

    {

      "max_instances": 10,

      "class_name": "MyContainer",

      "image": "./Dockerfile",

    },

  ],

  "durable_objects": {

    "bindings": [

      {

        "name": "MY_CONTAINER",

        "class_name": "MyContainer",

      },

    ],

  },

  "migrations": [

    {

      "tag": "v1",

      "new_sqlite_classes": ["MyContainer"],

    },

  ],

}


```

```

[[containers]]

max_instances = 10

class_name = "MyContainer"

image = "./Dockerfile"


[[durable_objects.bindings]]

name = "MY_CONTAINER"

class_name = "MyContainer"


[[migrations]]

tag = "v1"

new_sqlite_classes = [ "MyContainer" ]


```

Important points about this config:

* `image` points to a Dockerfile, to a directory containing a Dockerfile, or to a fully qualified image reference such as `registry.cloudflare.com/<YOUR_ACCOUNT_ID>/<IMAGE>:<TAG>`.
* `class_name` must be a [Durable Object class name](/durable-objects/api/base/).
* `max_instances` declares the maximum number of simultaneously running container instances that will run.
* The Durable Object must use [new\_sqlite\_classes](/durable-objects/best-practices/access-durable-objects-storage/#create-sqlite-backed-durable-object-class) not `new_classes`.

### The Container Image

Your container image must be able to run on the `linux/amd64` architecture, but aside from that, has few limitations.

<title></title>
<text>
## Bindings in Terraform

[Bindings](/workers/runtime-apis/bindings/) allow your Worker to interact with resources on the Cloudflare Developer Platform. In Terraform, bindings are configured differently than in Wrangler. Instead of separate top-level properties for each binding type (like `kv_namespaces`, `r2_buckets`, etc.), Terraform uses a single `bindings` array where each binding has a `type` property along with type-specific properties.

Below are examples of each binding type and their required properties:

### KV Namespace Binding

Bind to a [KV namespace](/kv/api/) for key-value storage:

```

bindings = [{

  type = "kv_namespace"

  name = "MY_KV"

  namespace_id = "your-kv-namespace-id"

}]


```

**Properties:**

* `type`: `"kv_namespace"`
* `name`: The variable name for the binding, accessible via `env.MY_KV`
* `namespace_id`: The ID of your KV namespace

### R2 Bucket Binding

Bind to an [R2 bucket](/r2/api/workers/workers-api-reference/) for object storage:

```

bindings = [{

  type = "r2_bucket"

  name = "MY_BUCKET"

  bucket_name = "my-bucket-name"

}]


```

**Properties:**

* `type`: `"r2_bucket"`
* `name`: The binding name to access via `env.MY_BUCKET`
* `bucket_name`: The name of your R2 bucket

### D1 Database Binding

Bind to a [D1 database](/d1/worker-api/) for SQL storage:

```

bindings = [{

  type = "d1"

  name = "DB"

  id = "your-database-id"

}]


```

**Properties:**

* `type`: `"d1"`
* `name`: The binding name to access via `env.DB`
* `id`: The ID of your D1 database

### Durable Object Binding

Bind to a [Durable Object](/durable-objects/api/) class:

```

bindings = [{

  type = "durable_object_namespace"

  name = "MY_DURABLE_OBJECT"

  class_name = "MyDurableObjectClass"

}]


```

**Properties:**


</text>

<title></title>
<text>
# Set this to today's date

compatibility_date = "2026-03-24"


[[env.production.r2_buckets]]

bucket_name = "screenshots-bucket"

binding = "screenshots_bucket"


[[env.staging.r2_buckets]]

bucket_name = "preview-screenshots-bucket"

binding = "screenshots_bucket"

remote = true


```

Running `wrangler dev -e staging` (or `CLOUDFLARE_ENV=staging vite dev`) with the above configuration means that:

* Your Worker code runs locally
* All calls made to `env.screenshots_bucket` will use the `preview-screenshots-bucket` resource, rather than the production `screenshots-bucket`.

### Recommended remote bindings

We recommend configuring specific bindings to connect to their remote counterparts. These services often rely on Cloudflare's network infrastructure or have complex backends that are not fully simulated locally.

<title></title>
<text>
## Remote bindings (beta) now works with Next.js — connect to remote resources (D1, KV, R2, etc.) during local development

Jun 30, 2025 

[ Workers ](/workers/) 

We [recently announced ↗](https://github.com/cloudflare/workers-sdk/discussions/9660) our public beta for [remote bindings](/workers/development-testing/#remote-bindings), which allow you to connect to deployed resources running on your Cloudflare account (like [R2 buckets](/r2) or [D1 databases](/d1)) while running a local development session.

Now, you can use remote bindings with your Next.js applications through the [@opennextjs/cloudflare adaptor ↗](https://opennext.js.org/cloudflare/bindings#remote-bindings) by enabling the experimental feature in your `next.config.ts`:

```

initOpenNextCloudflareForDev();

initOpenNextCloudflareForDev({

 experimental: { remoteBindings: true }

});


```

Then, all you have to do is specify which bindings you want connected to the deployed resource on your Cloudflare account via the `experimental_remote` flag in your binding definition:

* [  wrangler.jsonc ](#tab-panel-112)
* [  wrangler.toml ](#tab-panel-113)

```

{

  "r2_buckets": [

    {

      "bucket_name": "testing-bucket",

      "binding": "MY_BUCKET",

      "experimental_remote": true,

    },

  ],

}


```

```

[[r2_buckets]]

bucket_name = "testing-bucket"

binding = "MY_BUCKET"

experimental_remote = true


```


</text>

To enable remote bindings, add `"remote" : true` to each binding that you want to rely on a remote resource running on Cloudflare:  
   * [  wrangler.jsonc ](#tab-panel-242)  
   * [  wrangler.toml ](#tab-panel-243)  
```  
{  
  "name": "my-worker",  
  // Set this to today's date  
  "compatibility_date": "2026-03-31",  
  "r2_buckets": [  
    {  
      "bucket_name": "screenshots-bucket",  
      "binding": "screenshots_bucket",  
      "remote": true,  
    },  
  ],  
}  
```  
```  
name = "my-worker"  
# Set this to today's date  
compatibility_date = "2026-03-31"  
[[r2_buckets]]  
bucket_name = "screenshots-bucket"  
binding = "screenshots_bucket"  
remote = true  
```

## Mount R2 buckets in Containers

Nov 21, 2025 

[ Containers ](/containers/)[ R2 ](/r2/) 

[Containers](/containers/) now support mounting R2 buckets as FUSE (Filesystem in Userspace) volumes, allowing applications to interact with [R2](/r2/) using standard filesystem operations.

Common use cases include:

* Bootstrapping containers with datasets, models, or dependencies for [sandboxes](/sandbox/) and [agent](/agents/) environments
* Persisting user configuration or application state without managing downloads
* Accessing large static files without bloating container images or downloading at startup

FUSE adapters like [tigrisfs ↗](https://github.com/tigrisdata/tigrisfs), [s3fs ↗](https://github.com/s3fs-fuse/s3fs-fuse), and [gcsfuse ↗](https://github.com/GoogleCloudPlatform/gcsfuse) can be installed in your container image and configured to mount buckets at startup.

```

FROM alpine:3.20


# Install FUSE and dependencies

RUN apk update && \

    apk add --no-cache ca-certificates fuse curl bash


# Install tigrisfs

RUN ARCH=$(uname -m) && \

    if [ "$ARCH" = "x86_64" ]; then ARCH="amd64"; fi && \

    if [ "$ARCH" = "aarch64" ]; then ARCH="arm64"; fi && \


</text>
</result>
<result>
<url>https://developers.cloudflare.com/https://developers.cloudflare.com/containers/examples/r2-fuse-mount/</url>
<title></title>
<text>
R2_ENDPOINT="https://${R2_ACCOUNT_ID}.r2.cloudflarestorage.com"\n\

    echo "Mounting bucket ${R2_BUCKET_NAME}..."\n\

    /usr/local/bin/tigrisfs --endpoint "${R2_ENDPOINT}" -f "${R2_BUCKET_NAME}" /mnt/r2 &\n\

    sleep 3\n\

    \n\

    echo "Contents of mounted bucket:"\n\

    ls -lah /mnt/r2\n\

    ' > /startup.sh && chmod +x /startup.sh


EXPOSE 8080

CMD ["/startup.sh"]


```

The startup script creates a mount point, starts tigrisfs in the background to mount the bucket, and then lists the mounted directory contents.

### Passing credentials to the container

Your Container needs [R2 credentials](/r2/api/tokens/) and configuration passed as environment variables. Store credentials as [Worker secrets](/workers/configuration/secrets/), then pass them through the `envVars` property:

* [  JavaScript ](#tab-panel-1590)
* [  TypeScript ](#tab-panel-1591)

src/index.js

```

import { Container, getContainer } from "@cloudflare/containers";


export class FUSEDemo extends Container {

  defaultPort = 8080;

  sleepAfter = "10m";

  envVars = {

    AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID: this.env.AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID,

    AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY: this.env.AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY,

    R2_BUCKET_NAME: this.env.R2_BUCKET_NAME,

    R2_ACCOUNT_ID: this.env.R2_ACCOUNT_ID,

  };

}


```

src/index.ts

```

  FUSEDemo: DurableObjectNamespace<FUSEDemo>;

  AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID: string;

  AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY: string;

  R2_BUCKET_NAME: string;

  R2_ACCOUNT_ID: string;

}


export class FUSEDemo extends Container<Env> {

  defaultPort = 8080;

  sleepAfter = "10m";

  envVars = {

    AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID: this.env.AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID,

    AWS_SECRET_A

The R2 API includes some extensions and semantic differences from the S3 API. If you need S3 compatibility, consider using the [S3-compatible API](/r2/api/s3/).

## Concepts

R2 organizes the data you store, called objects, into containers, called buckets. Buckets are the fundamental unit of performance, scaling, and access within R2.

## Create a binding

Bindings

A binding is how your Worker interacts with external resources such as [KV Namespaces](/kv/concepts/kv-namespaces/), [Durable Objects](/durable-objects/), or [R2 Buckets](/r2/buckets/). A binding is a runtime variable that the Workers runtime provides to your code. You can declare a variable name in your Wrangler file that will be bound to these resources at runtime, and interact with them through this variable. Every binding's variable name and behavior is determined by you when deploying the Worker. Refer to [Environment Variables](/workers/configuration/environment-variables/) for more information.

A binding is defined in the Wrangler file of your Worker project's directory.

To bind your R2 bucket to your Worker, add the following to your Wrangler file. Update the `binding` property to a valid JavaScript variable identifier and `bucket_name` to the name of your R2 bucket:

* [  wrangler.jsonc ](#tab-panel-6294)
* [  wrangler.toml ](#tab-panel-6295)

```

{

  "r2_buckets": [

    {

      "binding": "MY_BUCKET", // <~ valid JavaScript variable name

      "bucket_name": "<YOUR_BUCKET_NAME>"

    
---

# Workers API reference

The in-Worker R2 API is accessed by binding an R2 bucket to a [Worker](/workers). The Worker you write can expose external access to buckets via a route or manipulate R2 objects internally.

The R2 API includes some extensions and semantic differences from the S3 API. If you need S3 compatibility, consider using the [S3-compatible API](/r2/api/s3/).

## Concepts

R2 organizes the data you store, called objects, into containers, called bucke

<title></title>
<text>
## Configure access to an R2 bucket

After your new R2 bucket is ready, use it inside your Worker application.

Use your R2 bucket inside your Worker project by modifying the [Wrangler configuration file](/workers/wrangler/configuration/) to include an R2 bucket [binding](/workers/runtime-apis/bindings/). Add the following R2 bucket binding to your Wrangler file:

* [  wrangler.jsonc ](#tab-panel-8879)
* [  wrangler.toml ](#tab-panel-8880)

```

{

  "r2_buckets": [

    {

      "binding": "MY_BUCKET",

      "bucket_name": "<YOUR_BUCKET_NAME>"

    }

  ]

}


```

```

[[r2_buckets]]

binding = "MY_BUCKET"

bucket_name = "<YOUR_BUCKET_NAME>"


```

Give your R2 bucket binding name. Replace `<YOUR_BUCKET_NAME>` with the name of the R2 bucket you created earlier.

Your Worker application can now access your R2 bucket using the `MY_BUCKET` variable. You can now perform CRUD (Create, Read, Update, Delete) operations on the contents of the bucket.

## Fetch from an R2 bucket

After setting up an R2 bucket binding, you will implement the functionalities for the Worker to interact with the R2 bucket, such as, fetching files from the bucket and uploading files to the bucket.


</text>

> AGENT

なるほど、**R2 FUSE マウント**ですね。Container 内から `/mnt/r2` のようにファイルシステムとして R2 に直接アクセスできます。tigrisfs を使う方式です。

これなら、dbt のアーティファクトを Container 内で直接 R2 にファイル書き込みできます。Worker 経由の HTTP 往復が不要になります。

設計を変更します:

1. **Container**: dbt 実行後、アーティファクト (`target/*.json`) を `/mnt/r2/` に直接コピー
2. **Worker**: コマンド発行のみ。アーティファクトは R2 から直接読み取り

{
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/transform/dbt-runner/Dockerfile",
  "content": "FROM python:3.12-slim\n\nWORKDIR /app\n\n# Install FUSE + tigrisfs for R2 mount\nRUN apt-get update && \\\n    apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends fuse curl ca-certificates && \\\n    ARCH=$(uname -m) && \\\n    if [ \"$ARCH\" = \"x86_64\" ]; then ARCH=\"amd64\"; fi && \\\n    curl -L \"https://github.com/tigrisdata/tigrisfs/releases/latest/download/tigrisfs-linux-${ARCH}\" \\\n      -o /usr/local/bin/tigrisfs && \\\n    chmod +x /usr/local/bin/tigrisfs && \\\n    apt-get clean && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*\n\n# Install uv\nCOPY --from=ghcr.io/astral-sh/uv:latest /uv /usr/local/bin/uv\n\n# Copy dependency files first for layer caching\nCOPY core/pyproject.toml core/uv.lock ./\n\nRUN uv sync --no-dev --frozen\n\n# Copy dbt project\nCOPY core/dbt_project.yml core/packages.yml core/profiles.yml ./\nCOPY core/models/ ./models/\nCOPY core/macros/ ./macros/\nCOPY core/seeds/ ./seeds/\nCOPY core/tests/ ./tests/\nCOPY core/analyses/ ./analyses/\nCOPY core/snapshots/ ./snapshots/\n\n# Install dbt packages\nRUN uv run dbt deps\n\n# Copy startup + HTTP server\nCOPY dbt-runner/startup.sh ./startup.sh\nCOPY dbt-runner/server.py ./server.py\nRUN chmod +x /app/startup.sh\n\nEXPOSE 8080\n\nCMD [\"/app/startup.sh\"]\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/transform/dbt-runner/server.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Lightweight HTTP server for dbt commands inside Cloudflare Container.\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport shutil\nimport subprocess\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, HTTPServer\n\nR2_MOUNT = \"/mnt/r2\"\nARTIFACT_PREFIX = \"dbt-core\"\n\n\nclass DbtHandler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):\n    def do_GET(self):\n        if self.path == \"/health\":\n            r2_mounted = os.path.ismount(R2_MOUNT)\n            self._json_response(200, {\"status\": \"ok\", \"r2_mounted\": r2_mounted})\n        else:\n            self._json_response(404, {\"error\": \"not found\"})\n\n    def do_POST(self):\n        if self.path in (\"/run\", \"/seed\", \"/test\", \"/build\"):\n            self._run_dbt(self.path.lstrip(\"/\"))\n        else:\n            self._json_response(404, {\"error\": \"not found\"})\n\n    def _run_dbt(self, command: str):\n        content_length = int(self.headers.get(\"Content-Length\", 0))\n        body = json.loads(self.rfile.read(content_length)) if content_length > 0 else {}\n\n        target = body.get(\"target\", \"dev\")\n        select = body.get(\"select\", None)\n        full_refresh = body.get(\"full_refresh\", False)\n\n        cmd = [\"uv\", \"run\", \"dbt\", command, \"--target\", target, \"--profiles-dir\", \".\"]\n        if select:\n            cmd.extend([\"--select\", select])\n        if full_refresh:\n            cmd.append(\"--full-refresh\")\n\n        result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, cwd=\"/app\")\n\n        # 成功時、アーティファクトを R2 に保存\n        artifacts_saved = []\n        if result.returncode == 0 and command in (\"run\", \"build\"):\n            artifacts_saved = self._save_artifacts_to_r2()\n\n        self._json_response(\n            200 if result.returncode == 0 else 500,\n            {\n                \"command\": command,\n                \"returncode\": result.returncode,\n                \"stdout\": result.stdout,\n                \"stderr\": result.stderr,\n                \"artifacts_saved\": artifacts_saved,\n            },\n        )\n\n    def _save_artifacts_to_r2(self) -> list[str]:\n        \"\"\"Copy dbt artifacts to R2 via FUSE mount.\"\"\"\n        if not os.path.ismount(R2_MOUNT):\n            return []\n\n        saved = []\n        artifact_files = [\n            \"target/manifest.json\",\n            \"target/run_results.json\",\n            \"target/catalog.json\",\n            \"target/sources.json\",\n        ]\n        timestamp = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime(\"%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ\")\n\n        for artifact in artifact_files:\n            src = os.path.join(\"/app\", artifact)\n            if not os.path.exists(src):\n                continue\n\n            filename = os.path.basename(artifact)\n\n            # latest/\n            latest_dir = os.path.join(R2_MOUNT, ARTIFACT_PREFIX, \"latest\")\n            os.makedirs(latest_dir, exist_ok=True)\n            shutil.copy2(src, os.path.join(latest_dir, filename))\n\n            # history/{timestamp}/\n            history_dir = os.path.join(R2_MOUNT, ARTIFACT_PREFIX, \"history\", timestamp)\n            os.makedirs(history_dir, exist_ok=True)\n            shutil.copy2(src, os.path.join(history_dir, filename))\n\n            saved.append(filename)\n\n        return saved\n\n    def _json_response(self, status: int, body: dict):\n        self.send_response(status)\n        self.send_header(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n        self.end_headers()\n        self.wfile.write(json.dumps(body).encode())\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    port = int(os.environ.get(\"PORT\", \"8080\"))\n    server = HTTPServer((\"0.0.0.0\", port), DbtHandler)\n    print(f\"dbt-runner server listening on port {port}\")\n    server.serve_forever()\n"
}

<title></title>
<text>
Mar 26, 2026
1. ### [Easily connect Containers and Sandboxes to Workers](/changelog/post/2026-03-26-outbound-workers/)  
[ Containers ](/containers/)  
[Containers](/containers/) and [Sandboxes](/sandbox/) now support connecting directly to Workers over HTTP. This allows you to call Workers functions and [bindings](/workers/runtime-apis/bindings/), like [KV](/kv) or [R2](/r2/), from within the container at specific hostnames.  
#### Run Worker code  
Define an `outbound` handler to capture any HTTP request or use `outboundByHost` to capture requests to individual hostnames and IPs.  
JavaScript  
```  
export class MyApp extends Sandbox {}  
MyApp.outbound = async (request, env, ctx) => {  
  // you can run arbitrary functions defined in your Worker on any HTTP request  
  return await someWorkersFunction(request.body);  
};  
MyApp.outboundByHost = {  
  "my.worker": async (request, env, ctx) => {  
    return await anotherFunction(request.body);  
  },  
};  
```  
In this example, requests from the container to `http://my.worker` will run the function defined within `outboundByHost`, and any other HTTP requests will run the `outbound` handler. These handlers run entirely inside the Workers runtime, outside of the container sandbox.  
TLS support coming soon  
Containers and Sandboxes currently only intercept HTTP traffic. HTTPS interception is coming soon. This will enable using Workers as a transparent proxy for credential injection.  
Even though this is just using HTTP, traffic to Workers is secure and runs on the same machine as the container. If needed, you can also upgrade requests to TLS from the Worker itself.  
#### Access Workers bindings  
Each handler has access to `env`, so it can call any binding set in [Wrangler config](/workers/wrangler/configuration/#bindings). Code inside the container makes a standard HTTP request to that hostname and the outbound Worker translates it into a binding call.

<title></title>
<text>
## Easily connect Containers and Sandboxes to Workers

Mar 26, 2026 

[ Containers ](/containers/) 

[Containers](/containers/) and [Sandboxes](/sandbox/) now support connecting directly to Workers over HTTP. This allows you to call Workers functions and [bindings](/workers/runtime-apis/bindings/), like [KV](/kv) or [R2](/r2/), from within the container at specific hostnames.

#### Run Worker code

Define an `outbound` handler to capture any HTTP request or use `outboundByHost` to capture requests to individual hostnames and IPs.

JavaScript

```

export class MyApp extends Sandbox {}


MyApp.outbound = async (request, env, ctx) => {

  // you can run arbitrary functions defined in your Worker on any HTTP request

  return await someWorkersFunction(request.body);

};


MyApp.outboundByHost = {

  "my.worker": async (request, env, ctx) => {

    return await anotherFunction(request.body);

  },

};


```

In this example, requests from the container to `http://my.worker` will run the function defined within `outboundByHost`, and any other HTTP requests will run the `outbound` handler. These handlers run entirely inside the Workers runtime, outside of the container sandbox.

TLS support coming soon

Containers and Sandboxes currently only intercept HTTP traffic. HTTPS interception is coming soon. This will enable using Workers as a transparent proxy for credential injection.

Even though this is just using HTTP, traffic to Workers is secure and runs on the same machine as the container. If needed, you can also upgrade requests to TLS from the Worker itself.

#### Access Workers bindings

Each handler has access to `env`, so it can call any binding set in [Wrangler config](/workers/wrangler/configuration/#bindings). Code inside the container makes a standard HTTP request to that hostname and the outbound Worker translates it into a binding call.

JavaScript

[ Containers ](/containers/)

<title></title>
<text>
## Defining outbound handlers

Use `outbound` to intercept outbound HTTP traffic regardless of destination:

JavaScript

```

import { Container, ContainerProxy } from "@cloudflare/containers";

export { ContainerProxy };


export class MyContainer extends Container {}


MyContainer.outbound = async (request, env, ctx) => {

  if (request.method !== "GET") {

    console.log(`Blocked ${request.method} to ${request.url}`);

    return new Response("Method Not Allowed", { status: 405 });

  }

  return fetch(request);

};


```

TLS support coming soon

Containers currently only intercept HTTP traffic. HTTPS interception is coming soon. This will enable using Workers as a transparent proxy for credential injection.

Even though this is just using HTTP, traffic to Workers is secure and runs on the same machine as the Container. If needed, you can also upgrade requests to TLS from the Worker itself.

Use `outboundByHost` to map specific domain names or IP addresses to handler functions:

JavaScript

```

import { Container, ContainerProxy } from "@cloudflare/containers";

export { ContainerProxy };


export class MyContainer extends Container {}


MyContainer.outboundByHost = {

  "my.worker": async (request, env, ctx) => {

    // Run arbitrary Workers logic from this hostname

    return await someWorkersFunction(request.body);

  },

};


```

The container calls `http://my.worker` and the handler runs entirely inside the Workers runtime, outside of the container sandbox.

If you define both, `outboundByHost` handlers take precedence over the catch-all `outbound` handler.

## Use Workers bindings in handlers

Outbound handlers have access to your Worker's bindings. Route container traffic to internal platform resources without changing application code.

JavaScript

```

export class MyContainer extends Container {}


MyContainer.outboundByHost = {

# Handle outbound traffic

Outbound Workers are Workers that handle HTTP requests made by your container. They act as programmable egress proxies, running on the same machine as the container with access to all Workers bindings.

Use outbound Workers to route requests to Workers functions and their bindings (KV, R2, Durable Objects, etc.)

## Defining outbound handlers

Use `outbound` to intercept outbound HTTP traffic regardless of destination:

JavaScript

```

import { Container, ContainerProxy } from "@cloudflare/containers";

export { ContainerProxy };


export class MyContainer extends Container {}

<title></title>
<text>
Mar 03, 2026
1. ### [Workflows step limit increased to 25,000 steps per instance](/changelog/post/2026-03-03-step-limits-to-25k/)  
[ Workflows ](/workflows/)[ Workers ](/workers/)  
Each Workflow on Workers Paid now supports 10,000 steps by default, configurable up to 25,000 steps in your `wrangler.jsonc` file:  
```  
{  
  "workflows": [  
    {  
      "name": "my-workflow",  
      "binding": "MY_WORKFLOW",  
      "class_name": "MyWorkflow",  
      "limits": {  
        "steps": 25000  
      }  
    }  
  ]  
}  
```  
Previously, each instance was limited to 1,024 steps. Now, Workflows can support more complex, long-running executions without the additional complexity of recursive or child workflow calls.  
Note that the maximum persisted state limit per Workflow instance remains **100 MB** for Workers Free and **1 GB** for Workers Paid. Refer to [Workflows limits](/workflows/reference/limits/) for more information.

Mar 03, 2026
1. ### [Real-time file watching in Sandboxes](/changelog/post/2026-03-03-sandbox-watch-file-events/)  
[ Agents ](/agents/)  
[Sandboxes](/sandbox/) now support real-time filesystem watching via `sandbox.watch()`. The method returns a [Server-Sent Events ↗](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/Server-sent%5Fevents) stream backed by native inotify, so your Worker receives `create`, `modify`, `delete`, and `move` events as they happen inside the container.  
#### `sandbox.watch(path, options)`  
Pass a directory path and optional filters. The returned stream is a standard `ReadableStream` you can proxy directly to a browser client or consume server-side.
</text>

<title></title>
<text>
#### API improvements  
We've also properly documented the [Workers Script And Version Settings](/api/resources/workers/subresources/scripts/subresources/script%5Fand%5Fversion%5Fsettings) in our public OpenAPI spec and SDKs.

Jun 18, 2025
1. ### [Remote bindings public beta - Connect to remote resources (D1, KV, R2, etc.) during local development](/changelog/post/2025-06-18-remote-bindings-beta/)  
[ Workers ](/workers/)  
Today [we announced the public beta ↗](https://github.com/cloudflare/workers-sdk/discussions/9660) of [remote bindings](/workers/development-testing/#remote-bindings) for local development. With remote bindings, you can now connect to deployed resources like [R2 buckets](/r2/) and [D1 databases](/d1/) while running Worker code on your local machine. This means you can test your local code changes against real data and services, without the overhead of deploying for each iteration.  
#### Example configuration  
To enable remote mode, add `"experimental_remote" : true` to each binding that you want to rely on a remote resource running on Cloudflare:  
   * [  wrangler.jsonc ](#tab-panel-534)  
   * [  wrangler.toml ](#tab-panel-535)  
```  
{  
  "name": "my-worker",  
  // Set this to today's date  
  "compatibility_date": "2026-03-31",  
  "r2_buckets": [  
    {  
      "bucket_name": "screenshots-bucket",  
      "binding": "screenshots_bucket",  
      "experimental_remote": true,  
    },  
  ],  
}  
```  
```  
name = "my-worker"  
# Set this to today's date  
compatibility_date = "2026-03-31"  
[[r2_buckets]]  
bucket_name = "screenshots-bucket"  
binding = "screenshots_bucket"  
experimental_remote = true  
```

> AGENT

これは素晴らしい！**Outbound Workers** (2026-03-26) を使えば、FUSE マウント不要で Container から直接 R2 バインディングにアクセスできます。Container 内の Python が `http://r2.worker` に HTTP リクエスト → Worker の `outboundByHost` が R2 バインディングで処理する構成です。

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
- **Outbound Workers** は Container の HTTP アウトバウンドを Worker がインターセプトするプロキシ機能
- Container 内のアプリは `http://r2.worker/put/key` のような単純な HTTP リクエストを送るだけ
- Worker 側で `outboundByHost` が `env.R2_BUCKET.put()` に変換 → R2 API キー不要、FUSE 不要
- FUSE より軽量で、Dockerfile に tigrisfs のインストールも不要になる
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

Outbound Workers に切り替えます。

> TOOL

{
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/transform/dbt-runner/Dockerfile",
  "content": "FROM python:3.12-slim\n\nWORKDIR /app\n\n# Install uv\nCOPY --from=ghcr.io/astral-sh/uv:latest /uv /usr/local/bin/uv\n\n# Copy dependency files first for layer caching\nCOPY core/pyproject.toml core/uv.lock ./\n\nRUN uv sync --no-dev --frozen\n\n# Copy dbt project\nCOPY core/dbt_project.yml core/packages.yml core/profiles.yml ./\nCOPY core/models/ ./models/\nCOPY core/macros/ ./macros/\nCOPY core/seeds/ ./seeds/\nCOPY core/tests/ ./tests/\nCOPY core/analyses/ ./analyses/\nCOPY core/snapshots/ ./snapshots/\n\n# Install dbt packages\nRUN uv run dbt deps\n\n# Copy HTTP server\nCOPY dbt-runner/server.py ./server.py\n\nEXPOSE 8080\n\nCMD [\"uv\", \"run\", \"python\", \"server.py\"]\n"
}

id: toolu_01PbSkGGLAU6C6Hy3dPoK6R1
```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/transform/dbt-runner/server.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Lightweight HTTP server for dbt commands inside Cloudflare Container.\n\nArtifacts are saved to R2 via Outbound Workers.\nContainer sends HTTP to http://r2.worker/{key} → Worker's outboundByHost\nintercepts and translates to R2 binding calls.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport subprocess\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, HTTPServer\nfrom urllib.request import Request, urlopen\n\nR2_HOST = \"http://r2.worker\"\n\n\nclass DbtHandler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):\n    def do_GET(self):\n        if self.path == \"/health\":\n            self._json_response(200, {\"status\": \"ok\"})\n        else:\n            self._json_response(404, {\"error\": \"not found\"})\n\n    def do_POST(self):\n        if self.path in (\"/run\", \"/seed\", \"/test\", \"/build\"):\n            self._run_dbt(self.path.lstrip(\"/\"))\n        else:\n            self._json_response(404, {\"error\": \"not found\"})\n\n    def _run_dbt(self, command: str):\n        content_length = int(self.headers.get(\"Content-Length\", 0))\n        body = json.loads(self.rfile.read(content_length)) if content_length > 0 else {}\n\n        target = body.get(\"target\", \"dev\")\n        select = body.get(\"select\", None)\n        full_refresh = body.get(\"full_refresh\", False)\n\n        cmd = [\"uv\", \"run\", \"dbt\", command, \"--target\", target, \"--profiles-dir\", \".\"]\n        if select:\n            cmd.extend([\"--select\", select])\n        if full_refresh:\n            cmd.append(\"--full-refresh\")\n\n        result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, cwd=\"/app\")\n\n        artifacts_saved: list[str] = []\n        if result.returncode == 0 and command in (\"run\", \"build\"):\n            artifacts_saved = self._save_artifacts_to_r2()\n\n        self._json_response(\n            200 if result.returncode == 0 else 500,\n            {\n                \"command\": command,\n                \"returncode\": result.returncode,\n                \"stdout\": result.stdout,\n                \"stderr\": result.stderr,\n                \"artifacts_saved\": artifacts_saved,\n            },\n        )\n\n    def _save_artifacts_to_r2(self) -> list[str]:\n        \"\"\"Upload dbt artifacts to R2 via Outbound Workers.\"\"\"\n        saved = []\n        artifact_files = [\n            \"target/manifest.json\",\n            \"target/run_results.json\",\n            \"target/catalog.json\",\n            \"target/sources.json\",\n        ]\n        timestamp = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime(\"%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ\")\n\n        for artifact in artifact_files:\n            src = os.path.join(\"/app\", artifact)\n            if not os.path.exists(src):\n                continue\n\n            filename = os.path.basename(artifact)\n\n            with open(src, \"rb\") as f:\n                data = f.read()\n\n            # PUT to latest/\n            self._put_r2(f\"dbt-core/latest/{filename}\", data)\n            # PUT to history/{timestamp}/\n            self._put_r2(f\"dbt-core/history/{timestamp}/{filename}\", data)\n            saved.append(filename)\n\n        return saved\n\n    def _put_r2(self, key: str, data: bytes):\n        \"\"\"PUT object to R2 via Outbound Worker at http://r2.worker.\"\"\"\n        req = Request(\n            f\"{R2_HOST}/{key}\",\n            data=data,\n            method=\"PUT\",\n            headers={\"Content-Type\": \"application/json\"},\n        )\n        try:\n            urlopen(req)\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Failed to PUT {key} to R2: {e}\")\n\n    def _json_response(self, status: int, body: dict):\n        self.send_response(status)\n        self.send_header(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n        self.end_headers()\n        self.wfile.write(json.dumps(body).encode())\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    port = int(os.environ.get(\"PORT\", \"8080\"))\n    server = HTTPServer((\"0.0.0.0\", port), DbtHandler)\n    print(f\"dbt-runner server listening on port {port}\")\n    server.serve_forever()\n"
}

Worker — `outboundByHost` で R2 バインディングにプロキシ + `ContainerProxy` エクスポート:

> TOOL

tool_use Write
id: toolu_01Pp6P7HQkt78gtx8PBrxQ7r
```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/ta93abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/transform/dbt-runner/src/index.ts",
  "content": "import { Container, ContainerProxy, getContainer } from \"@cloudflare/containers\";\n\n// Outbound Workers requires ContainerProxy export\nexport { ContainerProxy };\n\ninterface Env {\n  DBT_CONTAINER: DurableObjectNamespace<DbtContainer>;\n  DBT_ARTIFACTS: R2Bucket;\n  DATABRICKS_HOST: string;\n  DATABRICKS_HTTP_PATH: string;\n  DATABRICKS_TOKEN: string;\n}\n\nexport class DbtContainer extends Container<Env> {\n  defaultPort = 8080;\n  sleepAfter = \"5m\";\n\n  envVars = {\n    DATABRICKS_HOST: this.env.DATABRICKS_HOST,\n    DATABRICKS_HTTP_PATH: this.env.DATABRICKS_HTTP_PATH,\n    DATABRICKS_TOKEN: this.env.DATABRICKS_TOKEN,\n  };\n\n  override onStart() {\n    console.log(\"dbt-runner container started\");\n  }\n\n  override onStop() {\n    console.log(\"dbt-runner container stopped\");\n  }\n\n  override onError(error: unknown) {\n    console.error(\"dbt-runner container error:\", error);\n  }\n}\n\n// Outbound Workers: Container → http://r2.worker/{key} → R2 binding\nDbtContainer.outboundByHost = {\n  \"r2.worker\": async (request: Request, env: Env) => {\n    const url = new URL(request.url);\n    const key = url.pathname.slice(1); // strip leading /\n\n    if (request.method === \"PUT\") {\n      const body = await request.arrayBuffer();\n      await env.DBT_ARTIFACTS.put(key, body, {\n        httpMetadata: { contentType: \"application/json\" },\n      });\n      return new Response(\"OK\", { status: 200 });\n    }\n\n    if (request.method === \"GET\") {\n      const object = await env.DBT_ARTIFACTS.get(key);\n      if (!object) {\n        return new Response(\"Not Found\", { status: 404 });\n      }\n      return new Response(object.body, {\n        headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" },\n      });\n    }\n\n    return new Response(\"Method Not Allowed\", { status: 405 });\n  },\n};\n\nexport default {\n  async fetch(request: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {\n    const url = new URL(request.url);\n    const path = url.pathname;\n\n    if (path === \"/health\") {\n      return Response.json({ status: \"ok\" });\n    }\n\n    const container = getContainer(env.DBT_CONTAINER, \"dbt-runner-main\");\n\n    // dbt commands: POST /run, /seed, /test, /build\n    if (\n      request.method === \"POST\" &&\n      [\"/run\", \"/seed\", \"/test\", \"/build\"].includes(path)\n    ) {\n      const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));\n      const response = await container.fetch(`http://container${path}`, {\n        method: \"POST\",\n        headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" },\n        body: JSON.stringify(body),\n      });\n      return new Response(response.body, {\n        status: response.status,\n        headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" },\n      });\n    }\n\n    // アーティファクト取得 (R2 binding 経由)\n    if (path.startsWith(\"/artifacts/\")) {\n      const key = `dbt-core/${path.replace(\"/artifacts/\", \"\")}`;\n      const object = await env.DBT_ARTIFACTS.get(key);\n      if (!object) {\n        return Response.json({ error: \"not found\" }, { status: 404 });\n      }\n      return new Response(object.body, {\n        headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" },\n      });\n    }\n\n    return Response.json({ error: \"not found\" }, { status: 404 });\n  },\n};\n"
}

> AGENT

TypeCheck OK。最終構成のまとめ:

## アーキテクチャ

```
Client → Worker (fetch handler)
           ├── POST /run → Container (dbt run)
           │                  └── http://r2.worker/dbt-core/latest/manifest.json (PUT)
           │                         ↓ outboundByHost
           │                  Worker → env.DBT_ARTIFACTS.put()  ← R2 binding
           │
           └── GET /artifacts/latest/manifest.json
                    → env.DBT_ARTIFACTS.get()  ← R2 binding
```

**ポイント**:
- Container 内の Python は `http://r2.worker/{key}` に HTTP PUT するだけ
- Worker の `outboundByHost["r2.worker"]` がインターセプトして R2 バインディングで保存
- R2 API キーが Container に一切渡らない（Databricks Token のみ）
- FUSE/tigrisfs 不要 → Dockerfile が軽量

## ファイル一覧

```
transform/dbt-runner/
├── Dockerfile           # python:3.12-slim + uv + dbt project
├── server.py            # Container 内 HTTP サーバー (dbt + R2 outbound)
├── src/index.ts         # Worker + outboundByHost (R2 proxy)
├── wrangler.jsonc       # Containers + R2 binding
├── tsconfig.json
├── package.json         # pnpm
└── .gitignore
```

## デプロイに必要な手順

1. `wrangler r2 bucket create dbt-artifacts`
2. `wrangler secret put DATABRICKS_HOST`
3. `wrangler secret put DATABRICKS_HTTP_PATH`
4. `wrangler secret put DATABRICKS_TOKEN`
5. `pnpm deploy`

シークレットを登録します。3つ必要です:

- `DATABRICKS_HOST` → `dbc-f6e7e914-ce58.cloud.databricks.com`
- `DATABRICKS_HTTP_PATH` → `/sql/1.0/warehouses/643b66d8a22ac512`
- `DATABRICKS_TOKEN` → これは対話入力が必要

最初の2つは値が分かっているので、ターミナルで以下を実行してください:

```
! cd transform/dbt-runner && echo "dbc-f6e7e914-ce58.cloud.databricks.com" | pnpm wrangler secret put DATABRICKS_HOST && echo "/sql/1.0/warehouses/643b66d8a22ac512" | pnpm wrangler secret put DATABRICKS_HTTP_PATH
```

`DATABRICKS_TOKEN` はアクセストークンを入力する必要があるので:

```
! cd transform/dbt-runner && pnpm wrangler secret put DATABRICKS_TOKEN
```

> DEVELOPER

dbt-runner じゃなくて、coreの直下にDockerfileを置くのはダメ？

> AGENT

全然アリです。むしろ dbt プロジェクトが自分の Dockerfile を持つ方が自然です。

構成はこうなります:

```
transform/
├── core/                    # dbt プロジェクト + コンテナ定義
│   ├── Dockerfile           # ← ここに移動
│   ├── server.py            # ← ここに移動
│   ├── dbt_project.yml
│   ├── models/
│   └── ...
└── dbt-runner/              # Worker のみ (薄いプロキシ)
    ├── src/index.ts
    ├── wrangler.jsonc
    └── package.json
```

**メリット**: Dockerfile の COPY パスが `./` で済む（`core/` プレフィックス不要）。dbt プロジェクトとコンテナ定義が一体化。

> AGENT

OK。最終構成:

```
transform/
├── core/                    # dbt プロジェクト + コンテナ定義
│   ├── Dockerfile           # ビルドコンテキスト = core/ 自身
│   ├── server.py            # Container 内 HTTP サーバー
│   ├── dbt_project.yml
│   ├── profiles.yml
│   ├── models/
│   ├── macros/
│   ├── seeds/
│   └── ...
└── dbt-runner/              # Worker (薄いプロキシ)
    ├── src/index.ts         # outboundByHost で R2 proxy
    ├── wrangler.jsonc       # image: "../core"
    └── package.json
```

`wrangler.jsonc` の `"image": "../core"` がディレクトリ指定なので、自動的にその中の `Dockerfile` を使います。

> DEVELOPER

dbt-runner の他のファイルもcoreに置くのは？

> AGENT

Worker (TypeScript) と dbt プロジェクト (Python) が混在するのが気になるかどうかですね。

**技術的には問題ない**です。wrangler は `main: "src/index.ts"` で Worker コードを見つけるし、dbt は `dbt_project.yml` を起点に動くので干渉しません。

```
transform/core/
├── src/index.ts          # Worker
├── wrangler.jsonc        # Containers + R2 binding
├── package.json          # pnpm (Worker deps)
├── tsconfig.json
├── Dockerfile            # Container イメージ
├── server.py             # Container 内サーバー
├── dbt_project.yml       # dbt
├── pyproject.toml        # uv (dbt deps)
├── profiles.yml
├── models/
├── macros/
├── seeds/
└── ...
```

**懸念点**: `pnpm` と `uv` が同居、`package.json` と `pyproject.toml` が同じディレクトリに。管理が煩雑になる可能性はある。

どうします？個人プロジェクトだし割り切って1ディレクトリにまとめるか、今の Worker 分離を維持するか。

> AGENT

気になる点:

1. **`compatibility_date`** — `2025-04-01` は古い。Outbound Workers (2026-03-26) を使うなら最新にすべき
2. **`name`** — `dbt-runner` のまま。`dbt-core` の方がこのプロジェクトに合う？
3. **`compatibility_flags`** — `nodejs_compat` は Worker 側で Node.js API 使ってないので不要かも。ただし害もない

どう名付けたいですか？ `dbt-core`、`dbt-runner`、`core-dbt` など。それ以外はこちらで直します。

> AGENT

これらは全て **dbt_packages（外部パッケージ）** からの warnings です。自分のコードではありません。抑制するには `dbt_project.yml` で `warn-error-options` を設定するか、パッケージをアップデートするかです。

warnings の内訳:
- `CustomKeyInObjectDeprecation` — `dbt_project_evaluator` の typo (`descritption`)
- `PropertyMovedToConfigDeprecation` — `dbt_project_evaluator` の `meta` 位置
- `MissingArgumentsPropertyInGenericTestDeprecation` — `dbt_project_evaluator` のテスト形式

`dbt_project_evaluator` を最新版 (`1.2.3`) にアップデートすれば解消する可能性が高いです。

> AGENT

`PackageRedirectDeprecation` が1件残っています。`dbt_expectations` の内部依存 `calogica/dbt_date` が原因です。`dbt_date` も明示的に追加して解消するか、この1件は許容するかですが、これは `dbt_expectations` のパッケージ内の話なので制御できません。warning としては害がないのでこのまま進めます。

```
     1→"""Lightweight HTTP server for dbt commands inside Cloudflare Container.
     2→
     3→Artifacts are saved to R2 via Outbound Workers.
     4→Container sends HTTP to http://r2.worker/{key} → Worker's outboundByHost
     5→intercepts and translates to R2 binding calls.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→import json
     9→import os
    10→import subprocess
    11→from datetime import datetime, timezone
    12→from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, HTTPServer
    13→from urllib.request import Request, urlopen
    14→
    15→R2_HOST = "http://r2.worker"
    16→
    17→
    18→class DbtHandler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    19→    def do_GET(self):
    20→        if self.path == "/health":
    21→            self._json_response(200, {"status": "ok"})
    22→        else:
    23→            self._json_response(404, {"error": "not found"})
    24→
    25→    def do_POST(self):
    26→        if self.path in ("/run", "/seed", "/test", "/build"):
    27→            self._run_dbt(self.path.lstrip("/"))
    28→        else:
    29→            self._json_response(404, {"error": "not found"})
    30→
    31→    def _run_dbt(self, command: str):
    32→        content_length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", 0))
    33→        body = json.loads(self.rfile.read(content_length)) if content_length > 0 else {}
    34→
    35→        target = body.get("target", "dev")
    36→        select = body.get("select", None)
    37→        full_refresh = body.get("full_refresh", False)
    38→
    39→        cmd = ["uv", "run", "dbt", command, "--target", target, "--profiles-dir", "."]
    40→        if select:
    41→            cmd.extend(["--select", select])
    42→        if full_refresh:
    43→            cmd.append("--full-refresh")
    44→
    45→        result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, cwd="/app")
    46→
    47→        artifacts_saved: list[str] = []
    48→        if result.returncode == 0 and command in ("run", "build"):
    49→            artifacts_saved = self._save_artifacts_to_r2()
    50→
    51→        self._json_response(
    52→            200 if result.returncode == 0 else 500,
    53→            {
    54→                "command": command,
    55→                "returncode": result.returncode,
    56→                "stdout": result.stdout,
    57→                "stderr": result.stderr,
    58→                "artifacts_saved": artifacts_saved,
    59→            },
    60→        )
    61→
    62→    def _save_artifacts_to_r2(self) -> list[str]:
    63→        """Upload dbt artifacts to R2 via Outbound Workers."""
    64→        saved = []
    65→        artifact_files = [
    66→            "target/manifest.json",
    67→            "target/run_results.json",
    68→            "target/catalog.json",
    69→            "target/sources.json",
    70→        ]
    71→        timestamp = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ")
    72→
    73→        for artifact in artifact_files:
    74→            src = os.path.join("/app", artifact)
    75→            if not os.path.exists(src):
    76→                continue
    77→
    78→            filename = os.path.basename(artifact)
    79→
    80→            with open(src, "rb") as f:
    81→                data = f.read()
    82→
    83→            # PUT to latest/
    84→            self._put_r2(f"dbt-core/latest/{filename}", data)
    85→            # PUT to history/{timestamp}/
    86→            self._put_r2(f"dbt-core/history/{timestamp}/{filename}", data)
    87→            saved.append(filename)
    88→
    89→        return saved
    90→
    91→    def _put_r2(self, key: str, data: bytes):
    92→        """PUT object to R2 via Outbound Worker at http://r2.worker."""
    93→        req = Request(
    94→            f"{R2_HOST}/{key}",
    95→            data=data,
    96→            method="PUT",
    97→            headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"},
    98→        )
    99→        try:
   100→            urlopen(req)
   101→        except Exception as e:
   102→            print(f"Failed to PUT {key} to R2: {e}")
   103→
   104→    def _json_response(self, status: int, body: dict):
   105→        self.send_response(status)
   106→        self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
   107→        self.end_headers()
   108→        self.wfile.write(json.dumps(body).encode())
   109→
   110→
   111→if __name__ == "__main__":
   112→    port = int(os.environ.get("PORT", "8080"))
   113→    server = HTTPServer(("0.0.0.0", port), DbtHandler)
   114→    print(f"dbt-runner server listening on port {port}")
   115→    server.serve_forever()
   116→
```

}

     1→import { Container, ContainerProxy, getContainer } from "@cloudflare/containers";
     2→
     3→// Outbound Workers requires ContainerProxy export
     4→export { ContainerProxy };
     5→
     6→interface Env {
     7→  DBT_CONTAINER: DurableObjectNamespace<DbtContainer>;
     8→  DBT_ARTIFACTS: R2Bucket;
     9→  DATABRICKS_HOST: string;
    10→  DATABRICKS_HTTP_PATH: string;
    11→  DATABRICKS_TOKEN: string;
    12→}
    13→
    14→export class DbtContainer extends Container<Env> {
    15→  defaultPort = 8080;
    16→  sleepAfter = "5m";
    17→
    18→  envVars = {
    19→    DATABRICKS_HOST: this.env.DATABRICKS_HOST,
    20→    DATABRICKS_HTTP_PATH: this.env.DATABRICKS_HTTP_PATH,
    21→    DATABRICKS_TOKEN: this.env.DATABRICKS_TOKEN,
    22→  };
    23→
    24→  override onStart() {
    25→    console.log("dbt-runner container started");
    26→  }
    27→
    28→  override onStop() {
    29→    console.log("dbt-runner container stopped");
    30→  }
    31→
    32→  override onError(error: unknown) {
    33→    console.error("dbt-runner container error:", error);
    34→  }
    35→}
    36→
    37→// Outbound Workers: Container → http://r2.worker/{key} → R2 binding
    38→DbtContainer.outboundByHost = {
    39→  "r2.worker": async (request: Request, env: Env) => {
    40→    const url = new URL(request.url);
    41→    const key = url.pathname.slice(1); // strip leading /
    42→
    43→    if (request.method === "PUT") {
    44→      const body = await request.arrayBuffer();
    45→      await env.DBT_ARTIFACTS.put(key, body, {
    46→        httpMetadata: { contentType: "application/json" },
    47→      });
    48→      return new Response("OK", { status: 200 });
    49→    }
    50→
    51→    if (request.method === "GET") {
    52→      const object = await env.DBT_ARTIFACTS.get(key);
    53→      if (!object) {
    54→        return new Response("Not Found", { status: 404 });
    55→      }
    56→      return new Response(object.body, {
    57→        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    58→      });
    59→    }
    60→
    61→    return new Response("Method Not Allowed", { status: 405 });
    62→  },
    63→};
    64→
    65→export default {
    66→  async fetch(request: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {
    67→    const url = new URL(request.url);
    68→    const path = url.pathname;
    69→
    70→    if (path === "/health") {
    71→      return Response.json({ status: "ok" });
    72→    }
    73→
    74→    const container = getContainer(env.DBT_CONTAINER, "dbt-runner-main");
    75→
    76→    // dbt commands: POST /run, /seed, /test, /build
    77→    if (
    78→      request.method === "POST" &&
    79→      ["/run", "/seed", "/test", "/build"].includes(path)
    80→    ) {
    81→      const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));
    82→      const response = await container.fetch(`http://container${path}`, {
    83→        method: "POST",
    84→        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    85→        body: JSON.stringify(body),
    86→      });
    87→      return new Response(response.body, {
    88→        status: response.status,
    89→        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    90→      });
    91→    }
    92→
    93→    // アーティファクト取得 (R2 binding 経由)
    94→    if (path.startsWith("/artifacts/")) {
    95→      const key = `dbt-core/${path.replace("/artifacts/", "")}`;
    96→      const object = await env.DBT_ARTIFACTS.get(key);
    97→      if (!object) {
    98→        return Response.json({ error: "not found" }, { status: 404 });
    99→      }
   100→      return new Response(object.body, {
   101→        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
   102→      });
   103→    }
   104→
   105→    return Response.json({ error: "not found" }, { status: 404 });
   106→  },
   107→};
   108→
```

> AGENT

Container ロールアウト待ち中です。完了したら結果を確認します。

R2 バケットの最終構成はこうなります:

```
dbt-artifacts/
├── manifest.json        ← dbt run/build/docs で上書き
├── catalog.json         ← dbt docs generate で生成
├── run_results.json     ← dbt run/build で上書き
├── index.html           ← dbt docs generate で生成
└── sources.json         ← dbt run/build で生成（あれば）
```

API まとめ:

| メソッド | パス | 動作 |
|---------|------|------|
| GET | `/health` | ヘルスチェック |
| POST | `/seed` | `dbt seed` |
| POST | `/run` | `dbt run` + アーティファクト R2 保存 |
| POST | `/test` | `dbt test` |
| POST | `/build` | `dbt build` (seed+run+test) + R2 保存 |
| POST | `/docs` | `dbt docs generate` + R2 保存 |
| GET | `/docs` | index.html 配信 (R2 から) |
| GET | `/artifacts/{file}` | R2 からアーティファクト取得 |

Add a non-root user for running the application.

The container currently runs as root, which violates the principle of least privilege and increases security risk. If the application is compromised, an attacker would have root access to the container.

 FROM python:3.12-slim
 
 WORKDIR /app
 
 # Install uv
 COPY --from=ghcr.io/astral-sh/uv:latest /uv /usr/local/bin/uv
 
 # Copy dependency files first for layer caching
 COPY pyproject.toml uv.lock ./
 
 RUN uv sync --no-dev --frozen
 
 # Copy dbt project
 COPY dbt_project.yml packages.yml profiles.yml ./
 COPY models/ ./models/
 COPY macros/ ./macros/
 COPY seeds/ ./seeds/
 COPY tests/ ./tests/
 COPY analyses/ ./analyses/
 COPY snapshots/ ./snapshots/
 
 # Install dbt packages
 RUN uv run dbt deps
 
 # Copy HTTP server
 COPY server.py ./server.py
 
+# Create non-root user and switch to it
+RUN useradd -m -u 1000 appuser && \
+    chown -R appuser:appuser /app
+USER appuser
+
 EXPOSE 8080
 
 CMD ["uv", "run", "python", "server.py"]

     1→"""Lightweight HTTP server for dbt commands inside Cloudflare Container.
     2→
     3→Artifacts are saved to R2 via Outbound Workers.
     4→Container sends HTTP to http://r2.worker/{key} → Worker's outboundByHost
     5→intercepts and translates to R2 binding calls.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→import json
     9→import os
    10→import subprocess
    11→from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, HTTPServer
    12→from urllib.request import Request, urlopen
    13→
    14→R2_HOST = "http://r2.worker"
    15→
    16→
    17→class DbtHandler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    18→    def do_GET(self):
    19→        if self.path == "/health":
    20→            self._json_response(200, {"status": "ok"})
    21→        else:
    22→            self._json_response(404, {"error": "not found"})
    23→
    24→    def do_POST(self):
    25→        if self.path in ("/run", "/seed", "/test", "/build"):
    26→            self._run_dbt(self.path.lstrip("/"))
    27→        elif self.path == "/docs":
    28→            self._run_docs_generate()
    29→        else:
    30→            self._json_response(404, {"error": "not found"})
    31→
    32→    def _run_dbt(self, command: str):
    33→        content_length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", 0))
    34→        body = json.loads(self.rfile.read(content_length)) if content_length > 0 else {}
    35→
    36→        target = body.get("target", "dev")
    37→        select = body.get("select", None)
    38→        full_refresh = body.get("full_refresh", False)
    39→
    40→        cmd = ["uv", "run", "dbt", command, "--target", target, "--profiles-dir", "."]
    41→        if select:
    42→            cmd.extend(["--select", select])
    43→        if full_refresh:
    44→            cmd.append("--full-refresh")
    45→
    46→        result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, cwd="/app")
    47→
    48→        artifacts_saved: list[str] = []
    49→        if result.returncode == 0 and command in ("run", "build"):

    62→
    63→    def _run_docs_generate(self):
    64→        content_length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", 0))
    65→        body = json.loads(self.rfile.read(content_length)) if content_length > 0 else {}
    66→        target = body.get("target", "dev")
    67→
    68→        cmd = ["uv", "run", "dbt", "docs", "generate", "--target", target, "--profiles-dir", "."]
    69→        result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, cwd="/app")
    70→
    71→        artifacts_saved: list[str] = []
    72→        if result.returncode == 0:
    73→            artifacts_saved = self._save_docs_to_r2()
    74→
    75→        self._json_response(

    80→                "stdout": result.stdout,
    81→                "stderr": result.stderr,
    82→                "artifacts_saved": artifacts_saved,
    83→            },
    84→        )
    85→
    86→    def _save_docs_to_r2(self) -> list[str]:
    87→        """Upload dbt docs artifacts (manifest, catalog, index.html) to R2."""
    88→        saved = []
    89→        docs_files = [
    90→            ("target/manifest.json", "application/json"),
    91→            ("target/catalog.json", "application/json"),
    92→            ("target/index.html", "text/html"),
    93→            ("target/run_results.json", "application/json"),
    94→        ]
    95→
    96→        for artifact, content_type in docs_files:
    97→            src = os.path.join("/app", artifact)
    98→            if not os.path.exists(src):
    99→                continue
   100→
   101→            filename = os.path.basename(artifact)
   102→
   103→            with open(src, "rb") as f:
   104→                data = f.read()
   105→
   106→            self._put_r2(filename, data, content_type)
   107→            saved.append(filename)
   108→
   109→        return saved
   110→
   111→    def _save_artifacts_to_r2(self) -> list[str]:
   112→        """Upload dbt artifacts to R2 via Outbound Workers."""
   113→        saved = []
   114→        artifact_files = [
   115→            "target/manifest.json",
   116→            "target/run_results.json",
   117→            "target/catalog.json",
   118→            "target/sources.json",
   119→        ]
   120→        for artifact in artifact_files:
   121→            src = os.path.join("/app", artifact)
   122→            if not os.path.exists(src):
   123→                continue
   124→
   125→            filename = os.path.basename(artifact)
   126→
   127→            with open(src, "rb") as f:
   128→                data = f.read()
   129→
   130→            self._put_r2(filename, data)
   131→            saved.append(filename)
   132→
   133→        return saved
   134→
   135→    def _put_r2(self, key: str, data: bytes, content_type: str = "application/json"):
   136→        """PUT object to R2 via Outbound Worker at http://r2.worker."""
   137→        req = Request(
   138→            f"{R2_HOST}/{key}",
   139→            data=data,
   140→            method="PUT",
   141→            headers={"Content-Type": content_type},
   142→        )
   143→        try:
   144→            urlopen(req)
   145→        except Exception as e:
   146→            print(f"Failed to PUT {key} to R2: {e}")
   147→
   148→    def _json_response(self, status: int, body: dict):
   149→        self.send_response(status)
   150→        self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
   151→        self.end_headers()
   152→        self.wfile.write(json.dumps(body).encode())
   153→
   154→
   155→if __name__ == "__main__":
   156→    port = int(os.environ.get("PORT", "8080"))
   157→    server = HTTPServer(("0.0.0.0", port), DbtHandler)

outboundByHost\nintercepts and translates to R2 binding calls.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport subprocess\nfrom http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, HTTPServer\nfrom urllib.request         self._json_response(200, {\"status\": \"ok\"})\n        else:\n            self._json_response(404, {\"error\": \"not found\"})\n\n    def do_POST(self):\n        if self.path in (\"/run\", \"/seed\", \"/test\", \"/build\"):\n            self._run_dbt(self.path.lstrip(\"/\"))\n        elif self.path == \"/docs\":\n            self._run_docs_generate()\n        else:\n            self._json_response(404, {\"error\": \"not found\"})\n\n    def _parse_body(self) -> dict | None:\n        content_length = int(self.headers.get(\"Content-Length\", 0))\n        if content_length == 0:\n            return {}\n        try:\n            return json.loads(self.rfile.read(content_length))\n        except json.JSONDecodeError:\n            self._json_response(400, {\"error\": \"invalid JSON\"})\n            return None\n\n    def _run_dbt(self, command: str):\n        body = self._parse_body()\n        if body is None:\n            return\n\n        target = body.get(\"target\", \"dev\")\n        select = body.get(\"select\", None)\n        full_refresh = body.get(\"full_refresh\", False)\n\n        cmd = [\"uv\", \"run\", \"dbt\", command, \"--target\", target, \"--profiles-dir\", \".\"]\n        if select:\n            cmd.extend([\"--select\", select])\n        if full_refresh:\n            cmd.append(\"--full-refresh\")\n\n        try:\n            result = subprocess.run(\n                cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, cwd=\"/app\", timeout=DBT_TIMEOUT\n            )\n        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:\n \"command\": command,\n                \"returncode\": result.returncode,\n                \"stdout\": result.stdout,\n                \"stderr\": result.stderr,\n                \"artifacts_saved\": artifacts_saved,\n            },\n        )\n\n    def _run_docs_generate(self):\n        body = self._parse_body()\n        if body is None:\n            return\n        target = body.get(\"target\", \"dev\")\n\n        cmd = [\"uv\", \"run\", \"dbt\", \"docs\", \"generate\", \"--target\", target, \"--profiles-dir\", \".\"]\n\n        try:\n            result = subprocess.run(\n                cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, cwd=\"/app\", timeout=DBT_TIMEOUT\n            )\n        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:\n            self._json_response(504, {\"error\": \"dbt docs generate timed out\"})\n            return\n\n        artifacts_saved: list[str] = []\n        if result.returncode == 0:\n            artifacts_saved = self._save_docs_to_r2()\n\n        self._json_response(\n            200 if result.returncode == 0 else 500,\n            {\n                \"command\": \"docs generate\",\n                \"returncode\": result.returncode,\n                \"stdout\": result.stdout,\n                \"stderr\": result.stderr,\n                \"artifacts_saved\": artifacts_saved,\n            },\n        )\n\n    def _save_docs_to_r2(self) -> list[str]:\n        \"\"\"Upload dbt docs artifacts (manifest, catalog, index.html) to R2.\"\"\"\n        saved = []\n        docs_files = [\n            (\"target/manifest.json\", \"application/json\"),\n            (\"target/catalog.json\", \"application/json\"),\n            (\"target/index.html\", \"text/html\"),\n            (\"target/run_results.json\", \"application/json\"),\n        ]\n\n        for artifact, content_type in docs_files:\n            src = os.path.join(\"/app\", artifact)\n            if not os.path.exists(src):\n                continue\n\n            filename = os.path.basename(artifact)\n\n            with open(src, \"rb\") as f:\n                data = f.read()\n\n            if self._put_r2(filename, data, content_type):\n                saved.append(filename)\n\n        return saved\n\n    def _save_artifacts_to_r2(self) -> list[str]:\n        \"\"\"Upload dbt artifacts to R2 via Outbound Workers.\"\"\"\n        saved = []\n        artifact_files = [\n            \"target/manifest.json\",\n            \"target/run_results.json\",\n            \"target/catalog.json\",\n            \"target/sources.json\",\n        ]\n        for artifact in artifact_files:\n            src = os.path.join(\"/app\", artifact)\n            if not os.path.exists(src):\n                continue\n\n            filename = os.path.basename(artifact)\n\n            with open(src, \"rb\") as f:\n                data = f.read()\n\n            if self._put_r2(filename, data):\n                saved.append(filename)\n\n        return saved\n\n    def _put_r2(self, key: str, data: bytes, content_type: str = \"application/json\") -> bool:\n        \"\"\"PUT object to R2 via Outbound Worker at http://r2.worker.\"\"\"\n        req = Request(\n            f\"{R2_HOST}/{key}\",\n            data=data,\n            method=\"PUT\",\n            headers={\"Content-Type\": content_type},\n        )\n        try:\n            urlopen(req, timeout=30)\n            return True\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Failed to PUT {key} to R2: {e}\")\n            return False\n\n self.wfile.write(json.dumps(body).encode())\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    port = int(os.environ.get(\"PORT\", \"8080\"))\n    server = HTTPServer((\"0.0.0.0\", port), DbtHandler)\n > AGENT

全14件の CodeRabbit 指摘を修正してプッシュしました。

**修正サマリ:**

| カテゴリ | 修正内容 |
|---------|---------|
| **データ衝突** | prod schema を `staging` → `prod` に分離 |
| **Content-Type** | outbound GET で `httpMetadata.contentType` を使用 |
| **エラーハンドリング** | `_put_r2` が bool 返却、JSON パース 400、subprocess timeout |
| **セキュリティ** | GET メソッド制限、空キー検証、non-root ユーザー、uv バージョン固定 |
| **TypeScript** | `module: ESNext`、`esModuleInterop`、`isolatedModules` |

### Critical (3件)

| # | 内容 |
|---|------|
| 1 | **Worker に認証なし** — URL を知っていれば誰でも `dbt build --full-refresh` 実行可能 |
| 2 | **`select` パラメータ未検証** — dbt 引数インジェクションの可能性 |
| 3 | **Databricks Token のスコープ** — 最小権限の確認が必要 |

### High (3件)

| # | 内容 |
|---|------|
| 4 | `dim_dates.yml` の `day_of_week` ドキュメントが DuckDB のまま (0=Sunday) |
| 5 | `date_format('MMMM')` がロケール依存 |
| 6 | Container レスポンスの Content-Type を常に `application/json` に上書き |

### Medium (4件)

| # | 内容 |
|---|------|
| 7 | `_save_artifacts_to_r2` と `_save_docs_to_r2` の重複 |
| 8 | Dockerfile の `COPY analyses/ snapshots/` が空ディレクトリだと失敗する可能性 |
| 9 | `dbt_project.yml` の DuckDB 関連コメントが古い |
| 10 | `packages.yml` の DuckDB コメントが古い |

     8→  DBT_ARTIFACTS: R2Bucket;

    10→  DATABRICKS_HTTP_PATH: string;
    11→  DATABRICKS_TOKEN: string;
    12→}
    13→
    14→export class DbtContainer extends Container<Env> {
    15→  defaultPort = 8080;
    16→  sleepAfter = "5m";
    17→
    18→  envVars = {
    19→    DATABRICKS_HOST: this.env.DATABRICKS_HOST,
    20→    DATABRICKS_HTTP_PATH: this.env.DATABRICKS_HTTP_PATH,
    21→    DATABRICKS_TOKEN: this.env.DATABRICKS_TOKEN,
    22→  };
    23→

    29→    console.log("dbt-runner container stopped");
    30→  }

    33→    console.error("dbt-runner container error:", error);

    36→
    37→// Outbound Workers: Container → http://r2.worker/{key} → R2 binding
    38→DbtContainer.outboundByHost = {
    39→  "r2.worker": async (request: Request, env: Env) => {
    40→    const url = new URL(request.url);
    41→    const key = url.pathname.slice(1); // strip leading /
    42→
    43→    if (request.method === "PUT") {
    44→      const body = await request.arrayBuffer();
    45→      const contentType =
    46→        request.headers.get("Content-Type") || "application/json";
    47→      await env.DBT_ARTIFACTS.put(key, body, {
    48→        httpMetadata: { contentType },
    49→      });
    50→      return new Response("OK", { status: 200 });
    51→    }
    52→
    53→    if (request.method === "GET") {
    54→      const object = await env.DBT_ARTIFACTS.get(key);

    56→        return new Response("Not Found", { status: 404 });
    57→      }
    58→      const contentType =
    59→        object.httpMetadata?.contentType || "application/octet-stream";

    61→        headers: { "Content-Type": contentType },
    62→      });
    63→    }
    64→
    65→    return new Response("Method Not Allowed", { status: 405 });
    66→  },
    67→};

  "content": "import { Container, ContainerProxy, getContainer } from \"@cloudflare/containers\";\n\n// Outbound Workers requires ContainerProxy export\nexport { ContainerProxy };\n\ninterface Env {\n  DBT_CONTAINER: DurableObjectNamespace<DbtContainer>;\n  DBT_ARTIFACTS: R2Bucket;\n  DATABRICKS_HOST: string;\n  DATABRICKS_HTTP_PATH: string;\n  DATABRICKS_TOKEN: string;\n  API_KEY: string;\n}\n\nexport class DbtContainer extends Container<Env> {\n  defaultPort = 8080;\n  sleepAfter = \"5m\";\n\n  envVars = {\n    DATABRICKS_HOST: this.env.DATABRICKS_HOST,\n    DATABRICKS_HTTP_PATH: this.env.DATABRICKS_HTTP_PATH,\n    DATABRICKS_TOKEN: this.env.DATABRICKS_TOKEN,\n  };\n\n  override onStart() {\n    console.log(\"dbt-runner container started\");\n  }\n\n  override onStop() {\n    console.log(\"dbt-runner container stopped\");\n  }\n\n  override onError(error: unknown) {\n    console.error(\"dbt-runner container error:\", error);\n  }\n}\n\n// Outbound Workers: Container → http://r2.worker/{key} → R2 binding\nDbtContainer.outboundByHost = {\n  \"r2.worker\": async (request: Request, env: Env) => {\n    const url = new URL(request.url);\n    const key = url.pathname.slice(1); // strip leading /\n\n    if (request.method === \"PUT\") {\n      const body = await request.arrayBuffer();\n      const contentType =\n        request.headers.get(\"Content-Type\") || \"application/json\";\n      await env.DBT_ARTIFACTS.put(key, body, {\n        httpMetadata: { contentType },\n      });\n      return new Response(\"OK\", { status: 200 });\n    }\n\n    if (request.method === \"GET\") {\n      const object = await env.DBT_ARTIFACTS.get(key);\n      if (!object) {\n        return new Response(\"Not Found\", { status: 404 });\n      }\n      const contentType =\n        object.httpMetadata?.contentType || \"application/octet-stream\";\n      return new Response(object.body, {\n        headers: { \"Content-Type\": contentType },\n      });\n    }\n\n    return new Response(\"Method Not Allowed\", { status: 405 });\n  },\n};\n\nfunction authenticate(request: Request, env: Env): Response | null {\n  // GET /health, /docs, /artifacts は認証不要\n  if (request.method === \"GET\") return null;\n\n  const authHeader = request.headers.get(\"Authorization\");\n  if (!authHeader || authHeader !== `Bearer ${env.API_KEY}`) {\n    return Response.json({ error: \"unauthorized\" }, { status: 401 });\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n\nexport default {\n  async fetch(request: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {\n    const url = new URL(request.url);\n    const path = url.pathname;\n\n    if (path === \"/health\") {\n      return Response.json({ status: \"ok\" });\n    }\n\n    // POST エンドポイントは認証必須\n    const authError = authenticate(request, env);\n    if (authError) return authError;\n\n    const container = getContainer(env.DBT_CONTAINER, \"dbt-runner-main\");\n\n    // dbt commands: POST /run, /seed, /test, /build, /docs\n    if (\n      request.method === \"POST\" &&\n      [\"/run\", \"/seed\", \"/test\", \"/build\", \"/docs\"].includes(path)\n    ) {\n      const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));\n      const response = await container.fetch(`http://container${path}`, {\n        method: \"POST\",\n        headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" },\n        body: JSON.stringify(body),\n      });\n      const containerContentType =\n        response.headers.get(\"Content-Type\") || \"application/json\";\n      return new Response(response.body, {\n        status: response.status,\n        headers: { \"Content-Type\": containerContentType },\n      });\n    }\n\n    // dbt docs 配信 (R2 バケット直下から)\n    if (\n      request.method === \"GET\" &&\n      (path === \"/docs\" || path === \"/docs/\")\n    ) {\n      const object = await env.DBT_ARTIFACTS.get(\"index.html\");\n      if (!object) {\n        return Response.json(\n          { error: \"docs not generated yet. POST /docs first.\" },\n          { status: 404 }\n        );\n      }\n      return new Response(object.body, {\n        headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"text/html\" },\n      });\n    }\n\n    // アーティファクト取得 (R2 バケット直下)\n    if (request.method === \"GET\" && path.startsWith(\"/artifacts/\")) {\n      const key = path.replace(\"/artifacts/\", \"\");\n      if (!key) {\n        return Response.json({ error: \"not found\" }, { status: 404 });\n      }\n      const object = await env.DBT_ARTIFACTS.get(key);\n      if (!object) {\n        return Response.json({ error: \"not found\" }, { status: 404 });\n      }\n      const contentType =\n        object.httpMetadata?.contentType || \"application/octet-stream\";\n      return new Response(object.body, {\n        headers: { \"Content-Type\": contentType },\n      });\n    }\n\n    return Response.json({ error: \"not found\" }, { status: 404 });\n  },\n};\n"
}
