     1→"""Lightweight HTTP server for dbt commands inside Cloudflare Container.
     2→
     3→Artifacts are saved to R2 via Outbound Workers.
     4→Container sends HTTP to http://r2.worker/{key} → Worker's outboundByHost
     5→intercepts and translates to R2 binding calls.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→import json
     9→import os
    10→import subprocess
    11→from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, HTTPServer
    12→from urllib.request import Request, urlopen
    13→
    14→R2_HOST = "http://r2.worker"
    15→DBT_TIMEOUT = 3600  # 1 hour
    16→
    17→
    18→class DbtHandler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    19→    def do_GET(self):
    20→        if self.path == "/health":
    21→            self._json_response(200, {"status": "ok"})
    22→        else:
    23→            self._json_response(404, {"error": "not found"})
    24→
    25→    def do_POST(self):
    26→        if self.path in ("/run", "/seed", "/test", "/build"):
    27→            self._run_dbt(self.path.lstrip("/"))
    28→        elif self.path == "/docs":
    29→            self._run_docs_generate()
    30→        else:
    31→            self._json_response(404, {"error": "not found"})
    32→
    33→    def _parse_body(self) -> dict | None:
    34→        content_length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", 0))
    35→        if content_length == 0:
    36→            return {}
    37→        try:
    38→            return json.loads(self.rfile.read(content_length))
    39→        except json.JSONDecodeError:
    40→            self._json_response(400, {"error": "invalid JSON"})
    41→            return None
    42→
    43→    def _run_dbt(self, command: str):
    44→        body = self._parse_body()
    45→        if body is None:
    46→            return
    47→
    48→        target = body.get("target", "dev")
    49→        select = body.get("select", None)
    50→        full_refresh = body.get("full_refresh", False)
    51→
    52→        cmd = ["uv", "run", "dbt", command, "--target", target, "--profiles-dir", "."]
    53→        if select:
    54→            cmd.extend(["--select", select])
    55→        if full_refresh:
    56→            cmd.append("--full-refresh")
    57→
    58→        try:
    59→            result = subprocess.run(
    60→                cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, cwd="/app", timeout=DBT_TIMEOUT
    61→            )
    62→        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
    63→            self._json_response(504, {"error": f"dbt {command} timed out"})
    64→            return
    65→
    66→        artifacts_saved: list[str] = []
    67→        if result.returncode == 0 and command in ("run", "build"):
    68→            artifacts_saved = self._save_artifacts_to_r2()
    69→
    70→        self._json_response(
    71→            200 if result.returncode == 0 else 500,
    72→            {
    73→                "command": command,
    74→                "returncode": result.returncode,
    75→                "stdout": result.stdout,
    76→                "stderr": result.stderr,
    77→                "artifacts_saved": artifacts_saved,
    78→            },
    79→        )
    80→
    81→    def _run_docs_generate(self):
    82→        body = self._parse_body()
    83→        if body is None:
    84→            return
    85→        target = body.get("target", "dev")
    86→
    87→        cmd = ["uv", "run", "dbt", "docs", "generate", "--target", target, "--profiles-dir", "."]
    88→
    89→        try:
    90→            result = subprocess.run(
    91→                cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, cwd="/app", timeout=DBT_TIMEOUT
    92→            )
    93→        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
    94→            self._json_response(504, {"error": "dbt docs generate timed out"})
    95→            return
    96→
    97→        artifacts_saved: list[str] = []
    98→        if result.returncode == 0:
    99→            artifacts_saved = self._save_docs_to_r2()
   100→
   101→        self._json_response(
   102→            200 if result.returncode == 0 else 500,
   103→            {
   104→                "command": "docs generate",
   105→                "returncode": result.returncode,
   106→                "stdout": result.stdout,
   107→                "stderr": result.stderr,
   108→                "artifacts_saved": artifacts_saved,
   109→            },
   110→        )
   111→
   112→    def _save_docs_to_r2(self) -> list[str]:
   113→        """Upload dbt docs artifacts (manifest, catalog, index.html) to R2."""
   114→        saved = []
   115→        docs_files = [
   116→            ("target/manifest.json", "application/json"),
   117→            ("target/catalog.json", "application/json"),
   118→            ("target/index.html", "text/html"),
   119→            ("target/run_results.json", "application/json"),
   120→        ]
   121→
   122→        for artifact, content_type in docs_files:
   123→            src = os.path.join("/app", artifact)
   124→            if not os.path.exists(src):
   125→                continue
   126→
   127→            filename = os.path.basename(artifact)
   128→
   129→            with open(src, "rb") as f:
   130→                data = f.read()
   131→
   132→            if self._put_r2(filename, data, content_type):
   133→                saved.append(filename)
   134→
   135→        return saved
   136→
   137→    def _save_artifacts_to_r2(self) -> list[str]:
   138→        """Upload dbt artifacts to R2 via Outbound Workers."""
   139→        saved = []
   140→        artifact_files = [
   141→            "target/manifest.json",
   142→            "target/run_results.json",
   143→            "target/catalog.json",
   144→            "target/sources.json",
   145→        ]
   146→        for artifact in artifact_files:
   147→            src = os.path.join("/app", artifact)
   148→            if not os.path.exists(src):
   149→                continue
   150→
   151→            filename = os.path.basename(artifact)
   152→
   153→            with open(src, "rb") as f:
   154→                data = f.read()
   155→
   156→            if self._put_r2(filename, data):
   157→                saved.append(filename)
   158→
   159→        return saved
   160→
   161→    def _put_r2(self, key: str, data: bytes, content_type: str = "application/json") -> bool:
   162→        """PUT object to R2 via Outbound Worker at http://r2.worker."""
   163→        req = Request(
   164→            f"{R2_HOST}/{key}",
   165→            data=data,
   166→            method="PUT",
   167→            headers={"Content-Type": content_type},
   168→        )
   169→        try:
   170→            urlopen(req, timeout=30)
   171→            return True
   172→        except Exception as e:
   173→            print(f"Failed to PUT {key} to R2: {e}")
   174→            return False
   175→
   176→    def _json_response(self, status: int, body: dict):
   177→        self.send_response(status)
   178→        self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
   179→        self.end_headers()
   180→        self.wfile.write(json.dumps(body).encode())
   181→
   182→
   183→if __name__ == "__main__":
   184→    port = int(os.environ.get("PORT", "8080"))
   185→    server = HTTPServer(("0.0.0.0", port), DbtHandler)
   186→    print(f"dbt-runner server listening on port {port}")
   187→    server.serve_forever()
   188→
```

  "content": "\"\"\"Lightweight HTTP server for dbt commands inside Cloudflare Container.\n\nArtifacts are saved to R2 via Outbound Workers.\nContainer sends HTTP to http://r2.worker/{key} → Worker's self._run_dbt(self.path.lstrip(\"/\"))\n        elif self.path == \"/docs\":\n            self._run_docs_generate()\n        else:\n            self._json_response(404, {\"error\": \"not found\"})\n\n return json.loads(self.rfile.read(content_length))\n        except json.JSONDecodeError:\n            self._json_response(400, {\"error\": \"invalid JSON\"})\n            return None\n\n    def _validate_target(self, target: str) -> bool:\n        if target not in ALLOWED_TARGETS:\n            self._json_response(400, {\"error\": f\"invalid target: {target}. allowed: {ALLOWED_TARGETS}\"})\n            return False\n        return True\n\n    def _validate_select(self, select: str) -> bool:\n        if not SELECT_PATTERN.match(select):\n            self._json_response(400, {\"error\": f\"invalid select pattern: {select}\"})\n            return False\n        return True\n\n    def _run_dbt(self, command: str):\n        body = self._parse_body()\n        if body is None:\n self._json_response(504, {\"error\": f\"dbt {command} timed out\"})\n            return\n\n        artifacts_saved: list[str] = []\n        if result.returncode == 0 and command in (\"run\", \"build\"):\n            artifacts_saved = self._upload_artifacts([\n                (\"target/manifest.json\", \"application/json\"),\n                (\"target/run_results.json\", \"application/json\"),\n                (\"target/catalog.json\", \"application/json\"),\n                (\"target/sources.json\", \"application/json\"),\n            ])\n\n body = self._parse_body()\n        if body is None:\n            return\n\n        target = body.get(\"target\", \"dev\")\n        if not self._validate_target(target):\n            return\n\n        cmd = [\"uv\", \"run\", \"dbt\", \"docs\", \"generate\", \"--target\", target, \"--profiles-dir\", \".\"]\n\n        try:\n            result = subprocess.run(\n                cmd, out\"})\n            return\n\n        artifacts_saved: list[str] = []\n        if result.returncode == 0:\n            artifacts_saved = self._upload_artifacts(ARTIFACT_FILES)\n\n        self._json_response(\n            200 if result.returncode == 0 else 500,\n            {\n                \"command\": \"docs generate\",\n                \"returncode\": result.returncode,\n bytes, content_type: str = \"application/json\") -> bool:\n        \"\"\"PUT object to R2 via Outbound Worker at http://r2.worker.\"\"\"\n        req = Request(\n            f\"{R2_HOST}/{key}\",\n 全件修正してプッシュしました。`API_KEY` シークレットの登録が必要です:

```
! cd transform/core && pnpm wrangler secret put API_KEY
```

ランダムな文字列を設定してください。以降 POST リクエストには `Authorization: Bearer <API_KEY>` ヘッダーが必要になります。

> DEVELOPER

文字列を生成して登録して

tool_use Bash

  "description": "Generate and register API_KEY secret"
}
```

> AGENT

登録完了。API_KEY:

```
REDACTED
```

使い方:

```bash
# POST には Authorization ヘッダー必須
curl -X POST https://core.ta93abe.workers.dev/run \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer REDACTED" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{}'

# GET (docs, artifacts, health) は認証不要
curl https://core.ta93abe.workers.dev/docs
```

この API_KEY は安全な場所に保管してください。

--- gemini-code-assist[bot] on transform/core/server.py:181 ---
![medium](https://www.gstatic.com/codereviewagent/medium-priority.svg)

The `_put_r2` method catches all exceptions and only prints the error. This can lead to silent failures where the dbt command succeeds but artifacts are not saved. It is better to re-raise the exception or handle it in a way tha

--- Copilot on transform/core/src/index.ts:113 ---
The dbt execution endpoints (/run, /seed, /test, /build, /docs) are exposed without any authentication/authorization checks. This allows anyone who can reach the Worker to run dbt against your Databricks environment and write artifacts to R2. Add a bearer-token check (or equivalent) for POST endpoin

--- Copilot on transform/core/src/index.ts:128 ---
GET /docs only serves index.html, but dbt docs output also requires other files (e.g., manifest.json, catalog.json, and assets/*). Additionally, serving index.html at /docs (no trailing slash) can cause relative URLs like "assets/..." to resolve to "/assets/..." and 404. Consider redirecting /docs -

--- Copilot on transform/core/src/index.ts:63 ---
The outboundByHost GET path always responds with Content-Type: application/json, ignoring the stored object's metadata (e.g., index.html should be text/html, assets may be JS/CSS). Use the R2 object's httpMetadata (contentType) when present, and consider forwarding other relevant metadata headers (e

--- Copilot on transform/core/server.py:None ---
_save_docs_to_r2 only uploads a small fixed set of files and drops directory structure by using basename. dbt docs output typically includes an assets/ directory (JS/CSS/fonts) that index.html depends on, so the published docs are likely incomplete/broken. Update the upload logic to include the asse

--- Copilot on transform/core/server.py:81 ---
Request body parsing uses json.loads(...) without handling JSONDecodeError. A malformed or empty body will raise and terminate the request without a controlled JSON error response. Wrap JSON parsing in try/except and return a 400 with a clear error when the body is not valid JSON.

--- Copilot on transform/core/server.py:None ---
_put_r2 swallows upload failures (prints and continues), but callers still append the key to artifacts_saved, which can report successful uploads even when R2 PUT failed. Make _put_r2 return success/failure (or raise) and only include keys in artifacts_saved when the upload actually succeeded.

--- Copilot on transform/core/server.py:180 ---
urlopen(req) is called without a timeout and the response object isn't closed/read. This can hang the handler indefinitely on network issues and may leak resources. Set a reasonable timeout and ensure the response is closed (e.g., context manager) even when you don't need the body.

--- Copilot on transform/core/models/marts/core/dim_dates.sql:20 ---
dayofweek() in Databricks/Spark returns 1-7 (Sunday=1), whereas the previous DuckDB/Snowflake logic used a 0-6 style day index. If downstream models/tests expect 0=Sunday..6=Saturday, this is a breaking semantic change. Consider normalizing to the prior convention (e.g., subtracting 1) or explicitly

--- Copilot on transform/core/Dockerfile:None ---
The Dockerfile copies uv from ghcr.io/astral-sh/uv:latest. Using a floating "latest" tag makes container builds non-reproducible and can break unexpectedly when uv releases change. Pin the uv image/tag (or digest) to a known-good version to ensure deterministic builds.
```suggestion
COPY --from=ghcr

     1→import { Container, ContainerProxy, getContainer } from "@cloudflare/containers";
     2→
     3→// Outbound Workers requires ContainerProxy export
     4→export { ContainerProxy };
     5→
     6→interface Env {
     7→  DBT_CONTAINER: DurableObjectNamespace<DbtContainer>;
     8→  DBT_ARTIFACTS: R2Bucket;
     9→  DATABRICKS_HOST: string;
    10→  DATABRICKS_HTTP_PATH: string;
    11→  DATABRICKS_TOKEN: string;
    12→  API_KEY: string;
    13→}
    14→
    15→export class DbtContainer extends Container<Env> {
    16→  defaultPort = 8080;
    17→  sleepAfter = "5m";
    18→
    19→  envVars = {
    20→    DATABRICKS_HOST: this.env.DATABRICKS_HOST,
    21→    DATABRICKS_HTTP_PATH: this.env.DATABRICKS_HTTP_PATH,
    22→    DATABRICKS_TOKEN: this.env.DATABRICKS_TOKEN,
    23→  };
    24→
    25→  override onStart() {
    26→    console.log("dbt-runner container started");
    27→  }
    28→
    29→  override onStop() {
    30→    console.log("dbt-runner container stopped");
    31→  }
    32→
    33→  override onError(error: unknown) {
    34→    console.error("dbt-runner container error:", error);
    35→  }
    36→}
    37→
    38→// Outbound Workers: Container → http://r2.worker/{key} → R2 binding
    39→DbtContainer.outboundByHost = {
    40→  "r2.worker": async (request: Request, env: Env) => {
    41→    const url = new URL(request.url);
    42→    const key = url.pathname.slice(1); // strip leading /
    43→
    44→    if (request.method === "PUT") {
    45→      const body = await request.arrayBuffer();
    46→      const contentType =
    47→        request.headers.get("Content-Type") || "application/json";
    48→      await env.DBT_ARTIFACTS.put(key, body, {
    49→        httpMetadata: { contentType },
    50→      });
    51→      return new Response("OK", { status: 200 });
    52→    }
    53→
    54→    if (request.method === "GET") {
    55→      const object = await env.DBT_ARTIFACTS.get(key);
    56→      if (!object) {
    57→        return new Response("Not Found", { status: 404 });
    58→      }
    59→      const contentType =
    60→        object.httpMetadata?.contentType || "application/octet-stream";
    61→      return new Response(object.body, {
    62→        headers: { "Content-Type": contentType },
    63→      });
    64→    }
    65→
    66→    return new Response("Method Not Allowed", { status: 405 });
    67→  },
    68→};
    69→
    70→function authenticate(request: Request, env: Env): Response | null {
    71→  // GET /health, /docs, /artifacts は認証不要
    72→  if (request.method === "GET") return null;
    73→
    74→  const authHeader = request.headers.get("Authorization");
    75→  if (!authHeader || authHeader !== `Bearer ${env.API_KEY}`) {
    76→    return Response.json({ error: "unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
    77→  }
    78→  return null;
    79→}
    80→
    81→export default {
    82→  async fetch(request: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {
    83→    const url = new URL(request.url);
    84→    const path = url.pathname;
    85→
    86→    if (path === "/health") {
    87→      return Response.json({ status: "ok" });
    88→    }
    89→
    90→    // POST エンドポイントは認証必須
    91→    const authError = authenticate(request, env);
    92→    if (authError) return authError;
    93→
    94→    const container = getContainer(env.DBT_CONTAINER, "dbt-runner-main");
    95→
    96→    // dbt commands: POST /run, /seed, /test, /build, /docs
    97→    if (
    98→      request.method === "POST" &&
    99→      ["/run", "/seed", "/test", "/build", "/docs"].includes(path)
   100→    ) {
   101→      const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));
   102→      const response = await container.fetch(`http://container${path}`, {
   103→        method: "POST",
   104→        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
   105→        body: JSON.stringify(body),
   106→      });
   107→      const containerContentType =
   108→        response.headers.get("Content-Type") || "application/json";
   109→      return new Response(response.body, {
   110→        status: response.status,
   111→        headers: { "Content-Type": containerContentType },
   112→      });
   113→    }
   114→
   115→    // dbt docs 配信 (R2 バケット直下から)
   116→    if (
   117→      request.method === "GET" &&
   118→      (path === "/docs" || path === "/docs/")
   119→    ) {
   120→      const object = await env.DBT_ARTIFACTS.get("index.html");
   121→      if (!object) {
   122→        return Response.json(
   123→          { error: "docs not generated yet. POST /docs first." },
   124→          { status: 404 }
   125→        );
   126→      }
   127→      return new Response(object.body, {
   128→        headers: { "Content-Type": "text/html" },
   129→      });
   130→    }
   131→
   132→    // アーティファクト取得 (R2 バケット直下)
   133→    if (request.method === "GET" && path.startsWith("/artifacts/")) {
   134→      const key = path.replace("/artifacts/", "");
   135→      if (!key) {
   136→        return Response.json({ error: "not found" }, { status: 404 });
   137→      }
   138→      const object = await env.DBT_ARTIFACTS.get(key);
   139→      if (!object) {
   140→        return Response.json({ error: "not found" }, { status: 404 });
   141→      }
   142→      const contentType =
   143→        object.httpMetadata?.contentType || "application/octet-stream";
   144→      return new Response(object.body, {
   145→        headers: { "Content-Type": contentType },
   146→      });
   147→    }
   148→
   149→    return Response.json({ error: "not found" }, { status: 404 });
   150→  },
   151→};

(!key) {\n        return Response.json({ error: \"not found\" }, { status: 404 });\n      }\n      const object = await env.DBT_ARTIFACTS.get(key);\n      if (!object) {\n        if (key === \"index.html\") {\n          return Response.json(\n            { error: \"docs not generated yet. POST /docs first.\" },\n            { status: 404 }\n          );\n        }\n        return Response.json({ error: \"not found\" }, { status: 404 });\n      }\n      const contentType =\n        object.httpMetadata?.contentType || \"application/octet-stream\";\n      return new Response(object.body, {\n        headers: { \"Content-Type\": contentType },\n      });\n    }\n\n    // アーティファクト取得 (R2 バケット直下)\n    if (request.method === \"GET\" && path.startsWith(\"/artifacts/\")) {\n      const key = path.replace(\"/artifacts/\", \"\");\n      if (!key) {\n        return Response.json({ error: \"not found\" }, { status: 404 });\n      }\n      const object = await env.DBT_ARTIFACTS.get(key);\n      if (!object) {\n        return Response.json({ error: \"not found\" }, { status: 404 });\n      }\n      const contentType =\n        object.httpMetadata?.contentType || \"application/octet-stream\";\n      return new Response(object.body, {\n        headers: { \"Content-Type\": contentType },\n      });\n    }\n\n    return Response.json({ error: \"not found\" }, { status: 404 });\n  },\n};\n"
}
```

GitGuardian は pass、dbt Cloud が pending。他の CI がまだ開始していない可能性があります。

**対応済みの全指摘:**

| 指摘 | 対応 |
|------|------|
| 認証なし | Bearer token (API_KEY) |
| select/target 未検証 | regex + allowlist |
| `_put_r2` サイレント失敗 | bool 返却 |
| JSON パースエラー | Worker: 400, Container: 400 |
| subprocess timeout | 3600s |
| urlopen timeout | 30s |
| uv:latest | 0.11.2 固定 |
| non-root user | appuser |
| Content-Type | httpMetadata 使用 |
| /health がコンテナ無視 | container proxy + 503 |
| /docs サブパス未対応 | /docs/* ルーティング |
| request.json catch | 400 返却 |
| dev/prod スキーマ衝突 | prod schema 分離 |
| 古い DuckDB コメント | 更新 |
| .gitignore 不足 | Python/IDE/OS 追加 |

CI 通過を待ってマージしますか？
